English

Then $\operatorname{laplacianWithin}$ applied to $f$, $s$ and $x_{4}$ is an element of $F$ defined as $\operatorname{tensorIteratedFDerivWithinTwo}$ applied to $\Real$, $f$, $s$, $x_{4}$ and the canonical covariant tensor of $E$.
Formula
$f \in E \rightarrow F , s \in \wp E , x_{4} \in E \vdash \operatorname{laplacianWithin} (f , s , x_{4}):= \operatorname{tensorIteratedFDerivWithinTwo} (\Real , f , s , x_{4} , \operatorname{canonicalCovariantTensor} (E))\in F$

Wording
the continuous linear equivalence of coercive (from the name)
English
Assume that $B$ is coercive. Then the continuous linear equivalence of $\mathrm{coercive}$ is a continuous linear equivalence of $V$ and $V$ defined as the continuous linear equivalence of the continuous linear map of $B$.
Formula
$\mathrm{IsCoercive} (B)\vdash \operatorname{continuousLinearEquivOfBilin} (\mathrm{coercive}):= \operatorname{ofBijective} (\operatorname{continuousLinearMapOfBilin} (B), \mathrm{IsCoercive\_\_ker\_eq\_bot} (\mathrm{coercive}), \mathrm{IsCoercive\_\_range\_eq\_top} (\mathrm{coercive}))\in \mathrm{ContinuousLinearEquiv} (\operatorname{id} (\Real), V , V)$

English

Then $\operatorname{IsFormalAdjoint}$ holds for $T$ and $S$, if for all elements $x_{6}$ of $\SubtypeSet {x_{8}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoT {\operatorname{domain} (T)}{x_{8}})}$, for all elements $y$ of $\SubtypeSet {x_{7}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoT {\operatorname{domain} (S)}{x_{7}})}$, $\EqtMACRoH {\operatorname{inner} (x , \operatorname{toFun'} (T , x_{6}), y)}{\operatorname{inner} (x , x_{6} , \operatorname{toFun'} (S , y))}$.

Affine.Simplex.EquilateraldefMathlib.Analysis.Normed.Affine.Simplex, predicate
Wording
no wording
English
Let $s$ be a simplex of $P$ and $n$. Then $\operatorname{Equilateral}$ holds for $s$, if there exists a real number $r$, such that for all elements $i$ and $j$ of $\mathrm{Fin}$ applied to $n + 1$, if $i \neq j$, then the distance of the point of $s$ and $i$ and the point of $s$ and $j$ is equal to $r$.
Formula
$s \in \operatorname{Simplex} (R , P , n)\vdash \operatorname{Equilateral} (s):\Leftrightarrow \exists r \in \Real . \forall i \in \mathrm{Fin} (n + 1). \forall j \in \mathrm{Fin} (n + 1). i \neq j \rightarrow \operatorname{dist} (\operatorname{points} (s , i), \operatorname{points} (s , j))= r$
French
Soit $s$ un simplexe de $P$ et $n$. Alors $\operatorname{Equilateral}$ tient pour $s$ s'il existe un nombre réel $r$ tel que pour tous les éléments $i$ et $j$ de $\mathrm{Fin}$ appliqué à $n + 1$, si $i \neq j$, alors la distance du point de $s$ et $i$ et le point de $s$ et $j$ soit égale à $r$.
Affine.Simplex.RegulardefMathlib.Analysis.Normed.Affine.Simplex, predicate
Wording
s is regular (from the docstring)
English
Let $s$ be a simplex of $P$ and $n$. Then $s$ is regular, if for all permutations $x_{5}$ of $\mathrm{Fin}$ applied to $n + 1$, there exists an isometry equivalence $x_{6}$ of $P$ and $P$, such that $\operatorname{comp}$ applied to $\operatorname{points}$ applied to $s$ and $\operatorname{coe}$ applied to $x_{5}$ is equal to $\operatorname{comp}$ applied to $\operatorname{coe}$ applied to $x_{6}$ and $\operatorname{points}$ applied to $s$.
Formula
$s \in \operatorname{Simplex} (R , P , n)\vdash \operatorname{Regular} (s):\Leftrightarrow \forall x_{5} \in \operatorname{Perm} (\mathrm{Fin} (n + 1)). \exists x_{6} \in \mathrm{IsometryEquiv} (P , P). \EqtMACRoJ {\operatorname{comp} (\operatorname{points} (s), \operatorname{coe} (x_{5}))}{\operatorname{comp} (\operatorname{coe} (x_{6}), \operatorname{points} (s))}$
French

Mathlib.Analysis.Normed.Group.Hom

AddMonoidHom.mkNormedAddGroupHomdefMathlib.Analysis.Normed.Group.Hom, function or constant
Wording
the normed additive group homomorphism of f and C (from the name)
English
Let $f$ be an additive monoid homomorphism of $V$ and $W$. Let $C$ be a real number such that for all elements $v$ of $V$, the norm of $f$ applied to $v$ is less than or equal to the product of $C$ and the norm of $v$. Then the normed additive group homomorphism of $f$ and $C$ is a normed additive group homomorphism of $V$ and $W$.
Formula
$f \in \mathrm{AddMonoidHom} (V , W), C \in \Real , \forall v \in V . \operatorname{norm} (f (v))\leq C \times \operatorname{norm} (v)\vdash \operatorname{mkNormedAddGroupHom} (f , C , h)\in \mathrm{NormedAddGroupHom} (V , W)$
French
Soit $f$ un homomorphisme de monoïdes additif de $V$ et $W$. Soit $C$ un nombre réel tel que pour tous les éléments $v$ de $V$, la norme de $f$ appliqué à $v$ soit inférieure ou égale au produit de $C$ et de la norme de $v$. Alors l'homomorphisme de groupes additif normé de $f$ et $C$ est un homomorphisme de groupes additif normé de $V$ et $W$.

NormedAddGroupHom.SurjectiveOnWithdefMathlib.Analysis.Normed.Group.Hom, predicate
Wording
no wording
English
Let $f$ be a normed additive group homomorphism of $x$ and $x_{1}$. Let $K$ be an additive subgroup of $x_{1}$. Let $C$ be a real number. Then $\operatorname{SurjectiveOnWith}$ holds for $f$, $K$ and $C$, if for all elements $h$ of $x_{1}$, if $h$ is an element of $K$, then there exists an element $g$ of $x$, such that $\EqtMACRoH {f (g)}{h}$ and the norm of $g$ is less than or equal to the product of $C$ and the norm of $h$.
Formula
$f \in \mathrm{NormedAddGroupHom} (x , x_{1}), K \in \mathrm{AddSubgroup} (x_{1}), C \in \Real \vdash \operatorname{SurjectiveOnWith} (f , K , C):\Leftrightarrow \forall h \in x_{1} . \MembershipmemMACRoAA {K}{h}\rightarrow (\exists g \in x . \EqtMACRoJ {f (g)}{h}\wedge \operatorname{norm} (g)\leq C \times \operatorname{norm} (h))$
French

Alors $\operatorname{SurjectiveOnWith}$ tient pour $f$, $K$ et $C$ si pour tous les éléments $h$ de $x_{1}$, si $h$ est un élément de $K$, alors il existe un élément $g$ de $x$ tel que $\EqtMACRoH {f (g)}{h}$ et la norme de $g$ soit inférieure ou égale au produit de $C$ et de la norme de $h$.

the composition of g and f (from the name)

NormedAddGroupHom.opNormdefMathlib.Analysis.Normed.Group.Hom, function or constant
Wording
the operator norm of f (from the docstring)
English
Let $f$ be a normed additive group homomorphism of $x$ and $x_{1}$. Then the operator norm of $f$ is a real number defined as the infimum of $\operatorname{ofPred}$ applied to the function that maps $c$ to the proposition that $0 \leq c$ and for all elements $x_{4}$ of $x$, the norm of $f$ applied to $x_{4}$ is less than or equal to the product of $c$ and the norm of $x_{4}$.
Formula

French

Alors la norme d'opérateurs de $f$ est un nombre réel défini comme l'infimum de $\operatorname{ofPred}$ appliqué à la fonction qui envoie $c$ à la proposition que $0 \leq c$ et pour tous des éléments $x_{4}$ de $x$, la norme de $f$ appliqué à $x_{4}$ est inférieure ou égale au produit de $c$ et de la norme de $x_{4}$.

GroupNormstructureMathlib.Analysis.Normed.Group.Seminorm, type
Wording
group norm of G (from the name)
English
Let $G$ be a set. Then the group norms of $G$ are a basic type.
Formula
$G \in \mathrm{Set} \vdash \mathrm{GroupNorm} (G)\ \mathsf{set}$
French
Soit $G$ un ensemble. Alors les normes de groupes de $G$ sont un type de base.
GroupSeminormstructureMathlib.Analysis.Normed.Group.Seminorm, type
Wording
group seminorm of G (from the name)
English

GroupSeminorm.compdefMathlib.Analysis.Normed.Group.Seminorm, function or constant
Wording
the composition of p and f (from the name)
English
Let $p$ be a group seminorm of $E$. Let $f$ be a monoid homomorphism of $F$ and $E$. Then the composition of $p$ and $f$ is a group seminorm of $F$.
Formula
$p \in \mathrm{GroupSeminorm} (E), f \in \mathrm{MonoidHom} (F , E)\vdash \operatorname{comp} (p , f)\in \mathrm{GroupSeminorm} (F)$
French
Soit $p$ une semi-norme de groupes de $E$. Soit $f$ un homomorphisme de monoïdes de $F$ et $E$. Alors la composition de $p$ et $f$ est une semi-norme de groupes de $F$.
GroupSeminorm.toFundefMathlib.Analysis.Normed.Group.Seminorm, function or constant
Wording
the group seminorm of self and x1 (from the name)
English
Let $\mathrm{self}$ be a group seminorm of $G$. Let $x_{1}$ be an element of $G$. Then the group seminorm of $\mathrm{self}$ and $x_{1}$ is a real number defined as $\operatorname{proj0}$ applied to $\mathrm{self}$ and $x_{1}$.
Formula
$\mathrm{self} \in \mathrm{GroupSeminorm} (G), x_{1} \in G \vdash \operatorname{toFun} (\mathrm{self} , x_{1}):= \operatorname{proj0} (\mathrm{self} , x_{1})\in \Real$
French
Soit $\mathrm{self}$ une semi-norme de groupes de $G$. Soit $x_{1}$ un élément de $G$. Alors la semi-norme de groupes de $\mathrm{self}$ et $x_{1}$ est un nombre réel défini comme $\operatorname{proj0}$ appliqué à $\mathrm{self}$ et $x_{1}$.

NonarchAddGroupNormClassclassMathlib.Analysis.Normed.Group.Seminorm, predicate
Wording
no wording
English
Let $F$ be a set. Let $x$ be an element of $\mathrm{outParam}$ applied to the set of sets. Then we can say that $\mathrm{NonarchAddGroupNormClass}$ holds for $F$ and $x$.
Formula
$F \in \mathrm{Set} , x \in \mathrm{outParam} (\mathrm{Set})\vdash \mathrm{NonarchAddGroupNormClass} (F , x)\ \mathsf{prop}$
French
Soit $F$ un ensemble. Soit $x$ un élément de $\mathrm{outParam}$ appliqué à l'ensemble d'ensembles. Alors nous pouvons dire que $\mathrm{NonarchAddGroupNormClass}$ tient pour $F$ et $x$.

Mathlib.Analysis.Normed.Group.SeparationQuotient

SeparationQuotient.liftNormedAddGroupHomdefMathlib.Analysis.Normed.Group.SeparationQuotient, function or constant
Wording
the lift of f and hf (from the docstring)
English
Let $f$ be a normed additive group homomorphism of $M$ and $N$ such that for all elements $x_{2}$ of $M$, if the norm of $x_{2}$ is equal to $0$, then $\EqtMACRoH {f (x_{2})}{0}$. Then the lift of $f$ and $\mathrm{hf}$ is a normed additive group homomorphism of the set of separation quotients of $M$ and $N$.
Formula
$f \in \mathrm{NormedAddGroupHom} (M , N), \forall x_{2} \in M . \operatorname{norm} (x_{2})= 0 \rightarrow \EqtMACRoJ {f (x_{2})}{0}\vdash \operatorname{liftNormedAddGroupHom} (f , \mathrm{hf})\in \mathrm{NormedAddGroupHom} (\mathrm{SeparationQuotient} (M), N)$
French
Soit $f$ un homomorphisme de groupes additif normé de $M$ et $N$ tel que pour tous les éléments $x_{2}$ de $M$, si la norme de $x_{2}$ est égale à $0$, alors $\EqtMACRoH {f (x_{2})}{0}$. Alors le relèvement de $f$ et $\mathrm{hf}$ est un homomorphisme de groupes additif normé de l'ensemble de quotients de séparations de $M$ et $N$.
SeparationQuotient.liftNormedAddGroupHomEquivdefMathlib.Analysis.Normed.Group.SeparationQuotient, function or constant
Wording
the lift normed additive group homomorphism equivalence (from the name)
English
The lift normed additive group homomorphism equivalence is an element of $\EquivMACRoAE {(\SubtypeSet {f}{\mathrm{forall} (M , \lambda x_{2} . \mathrm{if} (\mathrm{Eq} (\operatorname{norm} (x_{2}), 0), \EqtMACRoH {f (x_{2})}{0}))})}{\mathrm{NormedAddGroupHom} (\mathrm{SeparationQuotient} (M), N)}$.
Formula
$\operatorname{liftNormedAddGroupHomEquiv} \in \EquivMACRoAL {(\SubtypeSet {f}{\mathrm{forall} (M , \lambda x_{2} . \mathrm{if} (\mathrm{Eq} (\operatorname{norm} (x_{2}), 0), \EqtMACRoJ {f (x_{2})}{0}))})}{\mathrm{NormedAddGroupHom} (\mathrm{SeparationQuotient} (M), N)}$
French
L'équivalence d'homomorphismes de groupes additive normée de relèvements est un élément de $\EquivMACRoAE {(\SubtypeSet {f}{\mathrm{forall} (M , \lambda x_{2} . \mathrm{if} (\mathrm{Eq} (\operatorname{norm} (x_{2}), 0), \EqtMACRoH {f (x_{2})}{0}))})}{\mathrm{NormedAddGroupHom} (\mathrm{SeparationQuotient} (M), N)}$.
SeparationQuotient.normedMkdefMathlib.Analysis.Normed.Group.SeparationQuotient, function or constant
Wording
no wording
English
$\operatorname{normedMk}$ is a normed additive group homomorphism of $M$ and the set of separation quotients of $M$.
Formula
$\operatorname{normedMk} \in \mathrm{NormedAddGroupHom} (M , \mathrm{SeparationQuotient} (M))$
French
$\operatorname{normedMk}$ est un homomorphisme de groupes additif normé de $M$ et l'ensemble de quotients de séparations de $M$.

Mathlib.Analysis.Normed.Group.Ultra

IsUltrametricDist.ball_openAddSubgroupdefMathlib.Analysis.Normed.Group.Ultra, function or constant
Wording
the ball open additive subgroup of S and hr (from the name)
English
Let $S$ be a set. Assume that $0 < r$. Then the ball open additive subgroup of $S$ and $\mathrm{hr}$ is an open additive subgroup of $S$.
Formula
$S \in \mathrm{Set} , 0 < r \vdash \mathrm{IsUltrametricDist\_\_ball\_openAddSubgroup} (S , \mathrm{hr})\in \mathrm{OpenAddSubgroup} (S)$
French
Soit $S$ un ensemble. Supposons que $0 < r$. Alors le sous-groupe additif ouvert de boules de $S$ et $\mathrm{hr}$ est un sous-groupe additif ouvert de $S$.
